| 1703 | **[mu-link] Slot lifecycle: a new tenant plays the previous app's unread audio.** P2 · logged by the main conversation from #1702, 2026-10-29 · Details: MuLinkSharedMemory.h:132-163, MuLinkClient.h:196-245, ServerEngine.h:443-462: claimSlot does not reset ring indices so a new tenant plays the previous app's unread audio (audible between apps); the client never re-checks active after the server reaps a slot; two producers on one SPSC ring after a >2 s stall. Shared-memory protocol change. · Updates: 2026-10-30 fix WRITTEN on the review-only laptop (commit 0a335b69), NOT built, NOT run. Protocol v5: a live slot's `active` now holds an owner token (0 = free); the server drains free slots' rings every block and a client claims a slot only when its ring is empty (fixes the new tenant playing the previous app's unread audio); the producer checks slot ownership before each fill pass and again before each ring write and stops with MuLinkClient::slotLost (bridge detaches and re-attaches) after a reap, and releaseSlot/heartbeat/preset name/program-change poll act only on a still-owned slot (fixes the client never re-checking active and two producers on one SPSC ring after a stall over 2 s). Residual: the few instructions between the final ownership check and the ring copy. 2026-10-30 Review: dsp-controller reviewed 0a335b69, Pass with notes (not Blocked, no compile errors, no real-time-safety defects). Fixed forward in b6df7df2 (not built or run): (1) narrow race where the server's per-block drain could discard a new tenant's first frames: the server now takes the ring write cursor BEFORE re-checking the slot is still free and discards only up to that cursor; (3) claimSlot tests readAvailable() != 0 (a racing negative count could pass > 0); (5) idle rings no longer stored to every block; (6) static_assert on ClientRegistry::slots alignment; (7) include <utility>. Accepted/left: (2) a freed slot is unclaimable until the server's next render block (bridge retries every 500 ms poll, so no latch; if the server device is stopped, a slot freed meanwhile stays unclaimable until it runs; confirm on the build PC); (4) a stale producer can still pass its final ownership check and write after a new claim in a few-instruction window (needs a stall landing exactly there; a full fix needs a per-ring generation field, a further protocol change, deliberately not done). New tests in mu-link SharedMemoryTests and ServerTests (stale audio not inherited, unique tokens, stale owner cannot free/write into the new tenant's slot, freed slot drained). Docs: docs/mu-link/design-mulink.md 'Slot lifecycle (protocol v5)'. · Needs: build + run the mu-link and mu-core unit tests on the build PC (cannot close from the laptop); test detach-then-attach with one free slot and with the server device stopped; mu-link and every product standalone must be rebuilt together (protocol v5 refuses a v4 peer); owner ranking. dsp-controller review DONE (Pass with notes) | 🔴 Open | — |

Design in docs/mu-tilate/design.md (ux-controller updating). Idea stage, nothing built. · Updates: 2026-10-30 Andy decided: layer type Phrase, layer preset .muPhrase, full preset .muTilate; 8 lanes; no MIDI play (self-driven sequencer); all formats (VST3 + CLAP + Standalone); must integrate into mu-Tronic automatically. 2026-10-30 correction by Andy: the 8 lanes decision is replaced; mu-Tilate has 8 layers like the other products, each with its own mixer strip and exactly one loop and one sample player (one loop per layer: decided yes); no multi-lane timeline. 2026-10-30 Andy: slices are monophonic per layer (the next slice cuts the previous via the declick fade); export wanted (Bounce to WAV and Export slices to a folder); needs a sample library mechanism like mu-Clid's, ideally generic in mu-core. 2026-10-30 Andy: ship some free loops (CC0/redistributable only) so presets can ship; tempo comes from file metadata first, then in-house analysis (exact-bar-length loop method over the onset curve); Export slices = one folder per loop; Bounce is Standalone-only for now, plugin formats left open. 2026-10-30 Andy: mu-Tilate has its own sample library (separate folder, setting and content from mu-Clid's hit samples; mu-Tilate's is longer phrases); memory: 8 loops standalone/plugin, 16 in mu-Tronic. 2026-10-30 Andy ruling: shared code always goes in mu-core unless there is a very good reason not to; mu-Tilate's shared mechanics (sample-library mechanism lifted out of mu-Clid, waveform/ruler/viewport, product-independent detectors, Bounce dialog) live in mu-core and mu-Clid is refactored onto them. Before scheduling: architecture-steward ruling on the byte budget / saved-state size policy only. 2026-10-30 Andy: time-stretch library chosen = Signalsmith Stretch (MIT; its notice goes in third-party credits); stretch is transient-weighted like Bitwig (attack plays unstretched, stretch increases with distance from the transient then plateaus uniformly), with controls for Transient hold, Curve (and maybe Strength). Needs a prototype to prove the varying-rate driving of the stretcher and a dsp-controller review before adoption. Scaffold created 2026-10-30: mu-tilate/ folders + CLAUDE.md and docs/mu-tilate/; no code, no CMake wiring; wiring checklist in mu-tilate/CLAUDE.md. 2026-10-30 Andy approved JUCE features not used yet (verified against JUCE 9.0.4 source), details in docs/mu-tilate/design-engine.md and design-library.md: (1) read WAV loop metadata acidBeats/acidTempo/aswgIsLoop/tracktionLoopInfo via AudioFormatReader::metadataValues for the tempo-metadata-first step; (2) juce::ThreadPool (9.0.2 addJob signature) for the detection worker; (3) juce::dsp::WindowingFunction with dsp::FFT for the spectral-flux detector; (4) evaluate juce::AudioThumbnail as backing cache for the mu-core zoomable waveform; (5) Interpolators::WindowedSinc for Re-pitch, needing a dsp-controller CPU review (8 layers / 16 in mu-Tronic). · Needs: none now (awaiting any new questions from the ux-controller) | 🔴 Open | — |

| 1625 | **[mu-core/audio] DSP audit minors, remaining (2026-10-16).** Logged by the main conversation at Andy's request from the second-pass read-only dsp-controller audit (static review, nothing built, no Blockers). (1) Risk until listened: Karplus-Strong loop gain up to 0.9999 with unity input can build +60 to +80 dB (KarplusStrongInsert.h:90-91); add a loop-gain-dependent trim or soft limiter; sustained-sine test at 50-100% feedback. (2) mu-On SampleChannel.h:48-64 hat/snare retrigger drops the old tail before the new onset (click at fast tempo); render the old tail up to the onset. (4) Channel/return/master level and pan are unsmoothed (MixerEngine.cpp:81-89); use `applyGainRamp` from the stored previous gain. (5) Insert noise seeds are not salted per instance (BitcrusherInsert.h:80, VocoderInsert.h:316 use kSeed ^ slot into an LCG; NoiseSeed.h documents why that is weak; oscillator noise uses the salt). (7) ADAA precision (SoftClipInsert.h:57, HardClipInsert.h:53: float with dx<1e-4 threshold, ~0.5% error near saturation, ~-46 dB noise); raise the threshold to >=1e-3 or use double. (8) Aliasing by design but unflagged: BassEngine.h:108-109 naive saw/square, XModOscPair.h:96 hard sync without minBLEP, WavetableOscillator.h:22 FM/PM index up to 8x with mip keyed to the base increment, FoldInsert.h no oversampling. (9) PhaserEffect.h:57 feedback near unity up to ~+40 dB with no limiter. (11) ToniVoice.h:121 `noteOn` resets both oscillator phases while the amp envelope may be in release (click); use a retrigger crossfade. (12) ToniVoice.h:166-167 glide sampled once per span. (13) MixerEngine.cpp:204-229 sidechain detector may read an already-ducked `channelBufs[src]` (chained sidechains are order-dependent); snapshot detectors first. (14) Cost items to measure on the build PC: VoiceEngine.cpp:172-178 `std::pow` per sample even with no active voice; filter/insert always run on silent input incl. Vocoder 20 bands x 3 biquads; CompressorLimiterInsert.h:85 `decibelsToGain` per sample at 0 dB; WavetableBank.cpp:349-356 `mipForInc` loop+division 4x per sample per voice (cache on frequency change); GatePattern.cpp:178 fmod+division per sample; BassEngine.h:95 filter runs idle; ChorusEffect.h:74 four identical delay lines and 32 % ops per sample. (16) No test coverage for disabled FX slots (#1618), stereo parameter-ramp parity (#1620), Vocoder spectral behaviour (#1621), SSB image rejection (#1622), oversize or 1-sample blocks (#1617), NaN recovery. **Audit not covered:** ProcessorBase_Render/LayerBlock/RetireBin internals, mu-clid sampler, mu-clid-midi engine, ToniScales/Chords/Scales, rest of MixerEngine.h, small filter headers, UI and persistence, Signalsmith reverb and JUCE filter internals (assumed from docs). Not built or run. On Hold: minor notes, revisit at the next stage boundary. **Behaviour-audit minors (2026-10-16)** (dsp-controller DSP-behaviour audits; code reading plus idealised re-implementations, nothing built, run or listened to; JUCE source unavailable). INSERTS: (a) Filter Drive stage adds DC (mu-core/Audio/MultiModeFilter.cpp:104, asymmetric tanh / y/(1-y); offset 0.0189 at amp 0.1 drive 1.0, 0.0454 at amp 0.5 drive 0.25, peak about 0.09 at amp 1.0 drive 0.25; nothing removes it unless HP or Lo Cut is on; thumps with the amp envelope; add a 5-20 Hz DC blocker). (b) Drive/Output smoothers in SoftClip/HardClip/Fold are separate linear ramps, so their product is not constant mid-ramp (about +28 dB for 15 ms moving Drive 0 to 100; modulating Drive pumps); smooth preGain only and compute outGain/preGain per sample, or smooth the log of both; the first block after reset ramps from 1.0 (related to #1626). (c) Bitcrusher default not transparent at 96/192 kHz (mu-core/Audio/InsertSlotConfig.h:189 Rate 48000 default; BitcrusherInsert.h:42 ratioF = sr/rate gives 2x/4x decimation with the AA one-pole at 21.6 kHz even at 16 bits and 0 dither); treat rate >= 0.5*sr as bypass or scale the range by sr/48000. (d) Bitcrusher AA is only 6 dB/oct at 0.45*rate (about -1 dB at the new Nyquist; aliasing item already logged) and bitAaFilter state is stale after a stretch with ratio <= 1, so it can click when the rate comes back down. (e) Comb filters have no level compensation (mu-core/Audio/Filters/FilterFamilies.h:195-203: feedback g = resonance; at res 0.99 Comb+ peak gain 1/(1-g) = +40 dB, +20 dB at res 0.9, Comb- peaks +40 dB); scale by (1-abs(g)) or sqrt(1-g^2); the ch1 getCurrentValue() smoothing issue there belongs to #1620. (f) Insert LPF hard switch at 19 kHz (mu-core/Audio/InsertProcessor.cpp:92-107: postDriveTone skipped at/above 19 kHz so its state is stale; a knob drag across 19 kHz can click; coefficient steps per block). (g) Compressor/Limiter detect on a mean-rectified envelope, so the threshold is about 4 dB above the label for a sine, and the channels are unlinked (separate env[ch], hard-panned hits shift the image); document or link the detectors with max(L,R). (i) StereoBiquadFilter swaps TDF-II coefficients at block boundaries so modulated cutoff zippers on Peak/Shelf/AP; OnePoleLP/HP are matched-z so the -3 dB point is not at the labelled cutoff above about sr/8 (-2.3 dB at 10 kHz at 44.1 kHz). (j) Compressor R channel reads end-of-block smoothed values (CompressorLimiterInsert.h:65-72); part of #1620. SEND FX / MIXER: (k) Chorus voices not panned and stereo width small (mu-core/Audio/FX/Send/ChorusEffect.h:72-83, 96-97; docs/design-fx.md:51 says voices are panned across the field by spread, but every voice goes to both channels and Spread only scales R modulation depth on odd voices; L/R correlation 1.000 at spread 0 for 2/3/4 voices, about 0.50 at spread 0.5-1.0 for 2 and 4 voices, about 0.67 for 3); fix with per-voice pan or opposite-phase L/R LFO scaled by spread. (l) Chorus at depth 100% (and spread above 0) drives the read delay to 0 (ChorusEffect.h:115 clamp jmax(2,d); minimum delay 0.0 samples at depth 1.0 spread 1.0 at 44.1 and 96 kHz with 4 voices; flat-topped LFO, flange-like comb, kink); clamp the swing to base - 2 samples; default depth 50% is safe. (o) Meter peaks overwritten each block instead of max-accumulated (mu-core/Audio/MixerEngine.cpp:51-64 storePeak; VUMeter polls at kUiRefreshHz 30 Hz, with 64-256 sample blocks the UI sees 1 block in 3 to 1 in 20, so short drum transients and clip-latch overs are missed); CAS-max on write and exchange(0) on UI read; test a one-block spike between polls. (q) Dirt reduces level (mu-core/Audio/FX/Slots/DelaySlot.cpp:150-152 tanh(x*g)/g with g = 1+8d: x=0.5 at dirt 1 gives 0.111 (-13.1 dB), dirt 0.5 -3.1 dB, x=0.1 dirt 1 -2.0 dB; ReverbSlot.cpp:118 input g = 1+4d: 0.5 at dirt 1 gives 0.197 (-8.1 dB)); add make-up or normalise by tanh(g). (r) Delay time smoothing causes a violent scrub on large time changes (DelaySlot.cpp:45, 74-75: 50 ms one-pole on delay samples; 250->500 ms initial slew 5.0 samples/sample i.e. read speed -4x reversed, 250->1000 ms -14x, feedback re-records it; every sync division/count/BPM jump gives a reversed pitched burst of about 100-150 ms); slew-limit to a small speed or crossfade two taps. (t) Reverb pre-delay steps with a click (see #1637). (u) echo_fb knob range 0..1 but the engine clamps at 0.98 (mu-core/Plugin/MixerFxParams.h:81 vs DelaySlot.h:41; top 2% dead); set the range to 0.98 as dly_fb already is. (v) RISK: intra-FX feeds are taken before the return fader (mu-core/Audio/FX/Slots/FXChain.cpp:22-42, applied at MixerEngine.cpp:311-403): Effect->Delay, Effect->Reverb and Delay->Reverb feed from the wet return buffer before return level, pan, mute, solo and duck, so muting or lowering the Effect return does not reduce what it feeds Delay/Reverb; the routing knobs live on the return strip so a user may expect post-fader; needs an architecture-steward ruling and a ux-controller strip note; test: mute ret_eff, eff2rev > 0, assert whether reverb still gets signal. (x) Factory wavetable level is inconsistent across tables and morph positions (mu-core/Audio/Wavetable/WavetableBank.cpp:167 peak-normalised; per-table RMS spread over 64 frames Basic -6.2..-1.4 dB, SawMorph -6.2..-2.0, Pulse -10.3..-1.4 (8.9 dB), FM Stack -4.9..-1.0, Formant -15.9..-10.3, Additive -13.3..-6.1 (7.2 dB); table to table up to about 14.5 dB (Formant vs Basic at default osc level); DC about 1e-16 so no DC issue); normalise each frame to a common RMS target (peak-limited) or per-table trim. (y) Wavetable mips have no oversampling headroom and use linear interpolation (WavetableBank.cpp:349-356 mipForInc takes the largest table with size/2 <= floor(0.5/inc): a 440 Hz note at 44.1 kHz allows 50 harmonics but gets the 64-sample table with 32 harmonics, 14 kHz limit vs 22 kHz; read at about 1.56 output samples per table sample with linear interpolation, droop sinc^2(k/64): k=16 -1.8 dB, k=24 -4.2 dB, k=32 -7.8 dB; mirror images fill the missing band); pick the mip one level up for 2x headroom or use cubic/Hermite; test an FFT of a rendered saw at 440 Hz vs ideal 1/k. (z) X-Mod AM not gain-compensated (mu-core/Audio/Wavetable/XModOscPair.h:118 a *= 1 + depth*b peaks at 2x (+6 dB) at depth +/-1, about +1.8 dB power for a sine modulator; RM at full depth is -3 dB RMS for two sines; sweeping Depth or flipping AM/RM/SSB changes level by several dB); normalise by 1/(1+abs(depth)) or document. (ab) mu-Tant applies voice controls only at block rate, unsmoothed (mu-tant/Source/Audio/SynthVoice.cpp:48-71: gain, osc1Gain, osc2Gain, noiseGain, osc positions, pitch, cutoff; only X-Mod index, depth and SSB shift are smoothed); a smooth LFO on level/position/pitch becomes a staircase at the host buffer rate and a stepped level change clicks; Risk depending on buffer size; test a 5 Hz LFO on Osc1 Level at block 2048. (ac) No oscillator frequency clamp (setFrequency and midiToFreq do not limit to Nyquist; mipForInc falls back to the 8-sample table, nothing blows up; Toni Root Octave 8 + Octaves 4 + Osc Octave +3 gives about 850 kHz and a folded pitch; Tant B, octave +3, +12 degrees, +24 st pitch env reaches about 17.8 kHz and any smooth Semi mod goes over Nyquist at 44.1 kHz); clamp inc below 0.45. (ad) Pink noise is 4.5 dB below white at the same Noise Level (measured RMS 0.344 vs 0.577 over 2M samples; Kellet coefficients correct, the *0.2 scale is peak-based); optionally rescale to match RMS. (ae) mu-Toni centre pan law: tv.pan always 0 so panL = panR = 0.707, -3 dB per channel before the mixer strip while Tant adds at unity; a mono bus adds L+R into one channel for +3 dB; gain staging only. (ag) Snare sample ends in a click (mu-on/Source/Audio/SampleChannel.h:80, 100-107: 300 ms buffer, noise time constant 120 ms, tone 100 ms, last-sample envelope about 0.057+0.025 (about -22 dB), SamplePlayer stops hard; hidden at default Decay 160 ms (about -38 dB at 300 ms) but at Decay 600 ms about -26 dB); make the buffer about 7 time constants or add a 5 ms end fade; test tail continuity at Decay 600. (ah) Most of the upper Hat and Snare Decay range does nothing (SampleChannel.h:34-35; ranges mu-on/Source/Plugin/PluginProcessor.cpp:63, 65: the gate can only shorten the sample; hat time constant 40 ms, time to -60 dB: Hat Decay 60 ms (default) 166 ms, 150 ms 218 ms, 400 ms (max) 251 ms, no gate 276 ms, so 150-400 ms changes the hat about 15% and an open hat is impossible; Snare range to 600 ms same); cap the range at the sample's natural length or let Decay stretch the sample; revisit with the planned .wav load. (ai) The top of the Swing knob is dead and the docs are off (mu-on/Source/Sequencer/GrooveSequencer.h:23 clamps swing to 0.9 but the parameter range is 0..1 (PluginProcessor.cpp:24); max offset 0.45 of a step while manual/design say up to half a step; ExtractionPinTests pins the 0.9 clamp); set the range to 0..0.9 or remove the clamp and update the pin. (aj) The Accent knob inverts below 0.72 (GrooveSequencer.h:24, 64: the knob sets the velocity of accented cells directly while plain cells are fixed at 0.72). **Decided by Andy 2026-10-20: REMAP, accentVel = 0.72 + knob*0.28.** A sound change: golden baselines need a deliberate refresh; implementation queued after the mu-Tronic Kick step 2b so it does not collide with the groove-row change there. (ak) Euclid patterns are maximally even but rotated against the canonical Toussaint/Bjorklund phase (mu-core/Sequencer/EuclideanGenerator.cpp:23-32 vs the mu-clid manual's 'Bjorklund's algorithm'; measured: E(3,8) generator x.x..x.. vs canonical x..x..x. (tresillo); E(5,8) xx.xx.x. vs x.xx.xx. (cinquillo); E(5,12) x.x.x..x.x.. vs x..x.x..x.x.; E(7,16) x.x.x.x..x.x.x.. vs x..x.x.x..x.x.x.; E(5,16), E(3,7), E(4,9) match); they are cyclic rotations so the rhythm is right but the downbeat phase differs: a user programming a tresillo hears hits at 0,2,5 not 0,3,6. Changing the generator silently re-times every saved project, so prefer documenting it or a 'classic phase' option; pin a table of (n,k) outputs; the E0 compat/golden corpora pin current behaviour. **2026-10-20: Andy did not understand the question; explained in plain terms (the generator plays E(3,8) as x.x..x.. where the textbook tresillo is x..x..x.; recommendation: switch to the canonical phase, since old presets sounding different does not matter). Awaiting Andy's one-line yes/no.** (al) Combined polyrhythm pattern cut at 256 steps when the cycle is longer (mu-core/Sequencer/Rhythm.cpp:42-44, 87-89 min(lcm,256); design says capped for display but audio uses the same cap; lengths such as 17 or 19 against 16 (LCM 272, 304) or 63 against 64 restart at step 256 with A and B phases reset, a phase glitch every 16 bars); cap only the display or document the limit. (am) mu-Toni Scale is inert when Snap is off and notes are not clamped to MIDI range (mu-toni/Source/Sequencer/Arp.cpp:91, ArpSequencer.h:145-147; the design 'root palette / quantise' (design-sequencer.md:88) is not implemented, Scale only affects diatonic snap which is off by default; stepMidi unclamped: Root octave 8 + 4-octave 13th chord reaches about MIDI 188, above Nyquist, aliased garbage); clamp 0..127 in stepMidi and either implement root quantise or update the manual line 'the key the arpeggio belongs to'. **Audit NOT covered:** no audio rendered or listened to, nothing built; JUCE source unavailable (JUCE StateVariableTPTFilter bandpass gain, ladder BPF24, ADSR::setParameters and SmoothedValue::reset claims need the build PC); Signalsmith reverb numerics (RT20 vs measured decay, width, level) need a render at the shipped Hall defaults; EchoEffect.h; oversampling around the saturators and cross-algorithm state hand-over on algo change; CPU cost (chorus 4 independent lines per voice, compressor log10/pow per sample per channel); Vocoder, EQ, RingMod, TapeSat verified correct except as logged; mu-Tant arp-less pieces, mu-clid insert FX and the mixer beyond the duck, hot-swap staging, UI code, docs/design-plugin-family.md modulation section beyond the matrix maths. **Review notes 2026-10-16:** the echo_fb knob range is 0..1 but the engine clamps at 0.98 and the frozen manifest pins 0..1, so changing it needs a deliberate manifest regeneration (left undone); EffectSlot has no live getter for algorithm parameters (EffectAlgorithmBase::getParam returns the definition default), which blocks a direct assertion in the #1632 test; ReverbSlot::updateReverb still takes a blocking SpinLock reachable from the audio thread (#1616 minor, not done); DelaySlot targetDelayL/R are plain floats written by setSpread/setDelayMs/setHostBpm (not done); tests still missing: Note-mode MIDI scan allocation on the real mu-Tant and mu-Clid processors, Bp24/Notch24 ladder gain, a live-value EffectSlot getter test. Updates: 2026-10-19 the other items were done (merged 2fd2d85; see git history); this row now lists only what remains. Review notes from that pass: an algorithm switch can replay stale delay-line audio (reset() in setAlgorithm would fix it, audible); activation shutdown join waits up to 12 s; 192 kHz delay buffers are about 13 MB per DelaySlot. 2026-10-20 Andy's answer on (ak) Euclid phase: YES, switch to the canonical Toussaint/Bjorklund phase. With (aj) Accent remap it forms a sound-change batch queued right after T1b 2c: pin an (n,k) table (E(3,8) = x..x..x.), update the mu-Clid manual wording if needed, re-record affected goldens as signed goldens-only commits. 2026-10-20 DONE (aj) Accent remap (accentVel = 0.72 + knob*0.28) and (ak) Euclid canonical Bjorklund phase (E(3,8) = x..x..x.), shipped together as one sound-change batch, pushed: mu-Clid 10 and mu-On 14 goldens re-recorded (goldens-only commits), Track fixture hashes updated (96e48b42), dsp minors added (9af3fdfc: level-table assert, no-realloc capacity pin, accentedVelocity(1.0)==1.0 pin); optional NaN guard on accent skipped; meta.baseline text in mu-Clid goldens is provenance only (not compared) and left as is; docs/design-sequencer.md Accent formula updated; dsp-controller signed the sound-change reason earlier. Most items were done 2026-10-19 (git history). Remaining: (a) `mu_ui::scale` (mu-core/UI/Components/MuLookAndFeel.h:37) is a global float, so changing UI scale in instance A mis-scales instance B; needs a per-shell UI design. (c) Item 7, risks with no failing case constructed: `getStateInformation` reads message-thread-only state without a lock (Rhythm::name, samples.path(i), LiveState, Pattern wavetable paths; mu-clid/Source/Persistence/PresetIO.cpp:640-650, mu-core/Control/LiveState.h:13) and some hosts call it off the message thread; `KnobWithLabel::grSource` dangling pointer (mu-core/UI/Voice/InsertSubsection.cpp:179, mu-core/UI/KnobWithLabel.cpp:254) after host `setStateInformation` shrinks the rhythm count with the editor open; `MuLinkClient::setPresetName` torn char[] (mu-core/Link/MuLinkClient.h:131); `MuLinkClient::detach` `stopThread(2000)` can force-kill the render thread; `lastEuclidOverrides` plain ints (mu-clid/Source/Plugin/PluginProcessor.h:224-246); MasterLoopSection timer calls `getRawParameterValue("mstrLoop")` by string per tick; mu-Clid legacy `restoreStateFromTree` holds layersLock across sample decode and file IO; NudgeInput raw-pointer editor deletion in callAsync (NudgeInput.cpp:203-240) can leak. Fragile note: the mu-clid commit swaps Rhythm contents, so editor pointers into `controlSequences` are safe only because the rebind runs synchronously before the outgoing object is destroyed; nothing may pump messages in between. | 1287 | **[mu-tant/timing] Under MIDI clock a gate edge near a pulse may close and reopen at a block edge (possible click).** Logged by: not recorded · The clock beat is pulse-quantised, so every block between two pulses gets the same start beat, while mu-Tant evaluates gates per sample from `blkBeatStart + i * bps`; at the next block edge its position can step back by up to a pulse, so a gate edge just before a pulse can be crossed twice. | ✅ Closed | 1184 |

| 1260 | **[mu-clid/timing] Under MIDI clock about 60 % of 16th-note steps fire one pulse late (about 21 ms at 120 BPM).** Logged by: not recorded · `MidiClockSync` builds the beat by adding 1.0 / 24.0 per pulse (MidiClockSync.h:79). 1/24 is not exact in binary, so after 6 pulses the beat is a hair below 0.25, and SequencerEngine.cpp:205 computes `(int)(beatPosition / StepLengthBeats)`, which floors that to the previous step; the step then… | ✅ Closed | 1160 |

| 1258 | **[family/midi] No Song Position Pointer in, and no Start / Stop / Continue / Song Position out.** Logged by: not recorded · Audit finding (whole tree searched). | 1256 | **[mu-link/latency] Each client rendered a full ring ahead (about 170 ms), so Stop, mutes, knob moves and preset changes answered late.** Logged by: not recorded · Producer filled the 8192-frame ring (MuLinkClient.h:164, MuLinkProtocol.h:57); sync was unaffected but live actions were heard about 170 ms late. | 1253 | **[family/sync] No sync offset, nudge or tap tempo, and tempo is whole-number only.** Logged by: not recorded · Audit finding. | 1252 | **[family/transport] A lost MIDI clock leaves the app-side slave "playing" on a frozen beat.** Logged by: not recorded · Audit finding. mu-link has a stall watchdog (ServerEngine.h:206) but the shared MidiClockSync.h the standalones use has none: if the cable is pulled or the master stops sending `0xF8` without a `0xFC`, `isPlaying_` stays true and the beat and tempo freeze. | ✅ Closed | 1175 |
| 1251 | **[family/transport] The host's time signature and bar start are ignored; bars are fixed at 4 beats.** Logged by: not recorded · Audit finding. The TransportBar position label hardcodes `beatsPerBar = 4` (TransportBar.cpp:224) and the sequencers and hot-swap boundaries count from PPQ 0 in fours. | ✅ Closed | 1169 |

| 358 | **[Audit]** `ReverbSlot::setParam` / `updateReverb` wrote `impl->reverb.roomMs`/`rt20`/`early`/`highDampRate`/`lowDampRate`/`detune`/`lowCutHz`/`highCutHz` directly from the message thread while `processReturn` ran `impl->reverb.process()` concurrently on the audio thread — undefined per typical embedded reverb single-threaded-access assumptions. Fix: introduced a `PendingParams` snapshot inside the pimpl, written by `updateReverb` (message thread) under a `juce::SpinLock` and gated by `pendingDirty` atomic. New `applyPendingReverbParams()` (audio thread) try-locks at the top of `processReturn` and copies the snapshot into the reverb's public fields BEFORE `reverb.process()` runs — single point where Signalsmith state is mutated. TryLock on contention defers one block instead of blocking audio. Also promoted `enabled`/`level`/`dirt`/`preDelay` to `std::atomic` for torn-read protection (they're read in the same processReturn / runPreDelay paths). `setAlgorithm` flow + `prepare` flow both still route through `updateReverb` so the first audio block applies the new snapshot. | ✅ Closed ||
| 357 | **[Audit].** Logged by: not recorded · `voiceEngines[r]->setActiveParams(modParams)` ran every block per rhythm at PluginProcessor.cpp:904 regardless of whether the modulation pass actually executed. | ✅ Closed | 438 |

| 355 | **[Audit].** Logged by: not recorded · `RhythmPanel::confirmReset` spun on `r.modLock.compare_exchange_weak` on the UI thread. If the audio thread was preempted while holding `modLock` (OS scheduler / DAW low-priority audio mode) the UI froze. | ✅ Closed | 440 |
